## 2026-11-08 — Media Gallery incomplete-output recovery + low-memory Create preview (SOURCE PARTIAL)

**Verified gap:** Media Gallery's existing 24-card/metadata preview could treat an empty or partially-written output as playable solely because its extension and filesystem attributes looked valid; the modal displayed one generic error and offered no way to recheck a file that had just completed or been moved back. The separately existing `CreateWorkspaceView.Result` decoded full-resolution 4K/8K images merely to show its 520px-high strip. Neither issue should trigger automatic re-generations, hidden network calls, or changes to the user's output.

**Implemented directly on `main`:** New pure `MediaGalleryOutputRecovery` distinguishes ready-on-disk, still generating/cancelling, job failed/cancelled, missing, zero-byte, inaccessible and unsafe/unsupported local paths, with actionable EN/IT guidance. Only **visible 24 cards** and an explicitly opened Preview perform filesystem health checks; indexing all artifacts remains pure path normalization without per-artifact filesystem probing. `MediaGalleryPreviewPolicy.KindFor` now blocks empty outputs and Core-reported running/failed/cancelled states before constructing an image/video decoder, shell opening or deleting. A `draft` artifact containing a nonempty supported file is still previewable (draft does not imply active generation). The details dialog exposes the persisted prompt, metadata and path even during failures and a user-initiated **Recheck local file / Ricontrolla il file** button; recheck releases any video decoder, rereads local status and reinstantiates a preview only when appropriate. It never relaunches generation or marks a job complete. The existing Gaming hard-off notification still pauses and clears a video source without auto-resume. Asynchronous image-decode errors now explicitly report an unavailable preview while retaining the original file.

**Create result usability + low-spec bound:** the result strip's `BitmapImage` uses `DecodePixelWidth = 1280` instead of decoding the full original frame. A new **Preview video** action opens a local `MediaPlayerElement` only on explicit click after the shared Gallery local-media admission; there is no autoplay or automatic generation. The existing Create view now releases that player when a new output is shown, when it is unloaded and upon a live Gaming state transition; the owner's original output remains unchanged. Open/export keep the full asset. The decode bound and manual player lifecycle are **not** a measured RAM/FPS gain or codec correctness proof.

**Tests authored, not executed:** new `MediaGalleryOutputRecoveryTests` covers active/failed/cancelled statuses, missing→reappeared, empty→nonempty, preserved draft preview, remote paths and EN/IT guidance; linked pure policy into Server xUnit project. Extended `ProfileSurfaceUiContractTests` for the recheck action, gating, source cleanup and Create decode bound. **Required native acceptance:** Windows App/Server build, focused `MediaGalleryOutputRecoveryTests|MediaGalleryPreviewPolicyTests|MediaGalleryBrowserTests|ProfileSurfaceUiContractTests`, full xUnit and exclusive FlaUI: 0-byte/partial/missing files, video not yet finalized, failure/cancel while dialog open, manual recheck after atomic rename, invalid codec/image decode failure, Create's manually requested video preview, unload and new output decoder teardown, Gaming entry/exit while both gallery modal and Create player are open, narrow 13-inch/200% DPI, keyboard/narrator and 4K/8K memory profile. A nonzero file is **not** proof its codec is valid or the write completed; hardware playback and image event tests remain unverified. Status: **PARTIAL**. No releases/tags/signatures/MSI/APK/Actions in remote session. Next: real bounded first-frame video thumbnail cache, persistent preview failure evidence, controller/TV UX and native low-spec instrumentation.

## 2026-11-08 — Media Gallery ownership-safe deletion + malformed-artifact recovery (SOURCE PARTIAL)

**Current code gap verified:** `MediaGalleryBrowser.CanDelete` previously returned true for every nonempty `ContentRef`, while the Gallery UI directly called `File.Delete` after a confirmation, regardless of where that artifact pointed. The existing `MediaGalleryDeleteCoordinator` checked item ID and path+file length+mtime, but **did not prove PaRuX ownership**. An external/user-managed file referenced by an imported or malformed artifact was therefore exposed to irreversible deletion. Gallery discovery also used unconditional `Path.GetFullPath` on every artifact reference: one malformed path/URL could abort subsequent batched artifact results. Open/Reveal/Edit buttons checked availability only when rendering a card, not again when clicked.

**Implemented on current `main` (source only):** Added pure `MediaGalleryDeletionPolicy`, and made `MediaGalleryBrowser.CanDelete` refuse deletion without explicit output roots. The Desktop/Portable Gallery enables and executes delete **only** for an existing supported image/video directly within the app's `GeneratedImagesDir`/`GeneratedVideosDir` or the owner's configured corresponding output folder. Exact normalized parent-folder equality (no prefix or nested matches), file extension/type checks (including contradictory ArtifactType/MIME/extension labels), and rejection of file/directory reparse points/junctions prevent accidental external-file deletion. Imported/external artifacts remain viewable but cannot be permanently deleted by the gallery; there is no new bulk deletion or implicit status mutation. UI checks ownership before showing the dialog and **again inside the existing coordinator-confirmed delete callback** after user confirmation. Existing file-identity check, cancel behavior and status update-after-success remain. The current FileIdentity still relies on path/length/mtime, not an OS stable file handle: native replacement/race acceptance remains required, and full atomicity is **not** claimed.

**Additional resilience/privacy:** Artifact loading now uses the **path-only, no-file-I/O** `MediaGalleryPreviewPolicy.TryGetLocalMediaPath` to normalize/deduplicate qualified local media references at 10k-item scale; it does not query file existence on every Artifact. Actual `KindFor` file attributes/type/link validation is reserved for visible thumbnails and user actions. Invalid/missing artifact records remain visible with diagnostics instead of halting the whole discovery batch. Card thumbnails use the same admission, and preview/open refuse symlink-file aliases. Reveal rejects UNC, URL, relative or malformed paths, while still allowing an owner to locate a missing local output's parent folder. Open, Reveal and Reuse as edit source all revalidate at click time rather than trusting their earlier enabled state. No unexpected file deletion, network fetch, model start or media decoder in background scan was added.

**Test sources authored, NOT executed:** new `MediaGalleryDeletionPolicyTests` exercises direct generated media, external registered artifacts, custom owner folder, prefix/sibling/nested trick paths, extension/MIME mismatches, missing/relative/UNC/URL paths and symlinks; expanded `MediaGalleryBrowserTests` for safe Reveal, `MediaGalleryPreviewPolicyTests` for no-I/O path-only dedup vs actual preview admission, and `ProfileSurfaceUiContractTests` for delete root wiring, post-confirmation enforcement and invalid artifact path recovery. Linked the pure policy in `PaRuX.Server.Tests.csproj`. **Mandatory Windows gates:** full App/Server build, focused `MediaGalleryDeletionPolicyTests|MediaGalleryFileIdentityTests|MediaGalleryBrowserTests|MediaGalleryPreviewPolicyTests|ProfileSurfaceUiContractTests`, full xUnit, exclusive FlaUI delete/decline/reconfirm/custom output imported artifacts, file removed or replaced between dialogs, NTFS reparse/junction/hardlink/UNC, 10k-artifact refresh with malformed paths, offline Core, 13-inch DPI/keyboard, and no egress. Hardlink alias and same-length+mtime replacement races need explicit follow-up; no claim of OS-atomic delete. This is **PARTIAL**, no release/tag/MSI/APK/signing/Actions from remote connector.

## 2026-11-07 — Consumer Create / Media Gallery on-demand previews (SOURCE PARTIAL)

**Verified gap in `main`:** Media Gallery already paged image thumbnails to 24 cards and limited decoded thumbnail resolution, but video cards were only placeholders and the **Details** dialog contained metadata with no playable preview. Previewing a video required launching an external player; eager per-item video decoding would regress memory/VRAM on low-spec PCs and Gaming hard-off. Artifact Workspace file references are not guaranteed safe local media paths: do not trust MIME metadata or execute an arbitrary file based on an `image` label.

**Implemented directly on `main`:** `MediaGalleryPreviewPolicy` admits only existing, absolute, **local** files with real image/video extensions; refuses URLs, UNC/device paths, relative/malformed paths, missing and executable/HTML/unsupported files. The gallery still builds only 24 thumbnail cards per page. Its **Preview** button (same existing AutomationId) now opens the existing Details dialog with a **lazy** <=960-pixel decoded image or `MediaPlayerElement` for local video; video never autoplays, has native transport controls, adapts to narrow dialogs, and releases/pause-clears its source when the dialog closes. Video previews are refused while Gaming owns the GPU; the modal subscribes to Gaming state changes and promptly pauses/releases its source when Gaming starts, without auto-resuming after exit. Errors retain prompt, metadata and file path for inspection. The existing `MediaGalleryBrowser.CanOpen` and `CanReuseAsEditSource` also use local-media admission, preventing a masquerading executable from being launched via the gallery or fed to image edit. No model start, new backend, background video decode, automatic network call or file mutation was introduced. Original Gallery item metadata/listing and Delete semantics are unchanged.

**Tests authored, not executed:** new `MediaGalleryPreviewPolicyTests` (local image/video, deceptive extension, URL/UNC/relative/missing/malformed, intact unsupported file), expanded `MediaGalleryBrowserTests` (reject executable shell-open/edit) and `ProfileSurfaceUiContractTests` (on-demand source wiring, no autoplay, bounded decode, decoder teardown, accessible Preview action). Linked policy source into Server xUnit project. **Windows gate:** compile .NET App/Server/Contracts; focused `MediaGalleryPreviewPolicyTests|MediaGalleryBrowserTests|ProfileSurfaceUiContractTests`, full xUnit and exclusive FlaUI: actual PNG/JPEG/WebP + 4K/8K decoded image peak memory; playable MP4/WebM/MOV, invalid codec/partial video/removed file; verify pause and GPU/media decoder release after dialog close; 10k-gallery paging, refresh races, 13-inch/125–200% DPI and 4K/HDR accessibility, Core disconnect/Gaming hard-off, no unexpected network. A video player shown by code does **not** prove a real codec works or preview performance. Server-side video still thumbnail/first-frame extraction remains **MISSING**, not a claim of competitor parity. No release, tag, build/signature or GitHub Actions remotely.

## 2026-11-07 — Model Hub safe defaults and manual GGUF continuity (SOURCE PARTIAL)

**Gap confirmed in current code:** Model Hub search initialized every repository with the generic `PreferredBalancedQuantChoiceIndex` and only replaced it when the hardware-aware policy returned a suitable quantisation. If the Core hardware probe was missing, the backend was ineligible, or no file met the quality/memory floor, the old generic selection remained enabled as the default **Review download** path. It looked like a recommendation without a defensible fit. Separately, `ModelHubQuantAdvice.Build` discarded an explicitly pinned file whenever hardware detection was unavailable; if a pinned set vanished from the refreshed list, its replacement could be selected automatically.

**Source implementation on `main`:** Only an explicit first-run setup handoff retains its prior preferred file. Regular search now leaves the default unselected unless `ModelHubQuantAdvice` returns an actually downloadable, quality-floor-qualified choice. When none exists, the card explains the limitation in EN/IT and directs the user to **Quantization options**, where the dialog requires deliberate selection before opening the existing source/size/licence review. Nothing is automatically downloaded, loaded or benchmarked. The variant dialog now retains the explicitly reviewed set name **per repository in the current UI session** and feeds it back into the policy on search refresh (no persistent filesystem paths). A matching manual GGUF pin survives absent hardware discovery with `Unknown` placement; a missing pin fails closed, without silently switching to another file. Existing choice/owner/Gaming gates are unchanged.

**Tests authored, NOT EXECUTED:** `ModelHubQuantAdviceTests` covers pin persistence with no hardware, stale pin, and 4 GiB no-fit; `ProfileSurfaceUiContractTests` checks safe search fallback and manual picker wiring. **P1 Windows gate:** build Contracts/Intelligence/Server/App and run focused/full xUnit, then exclusive FlaUI Model Hub with missing/invalid Core probe, CPU-only 4/8/16 GiB, eligible/ineligible GPU, UMA, a missing pinned set, first-use handoff and cancel/review flows at 13-inch/200% DPI in EN/IT. Confirm no unwanted network request, auto-selection, memory overcommit, or Gaming runtime use. No native execution, release, tag, MSI, APK, signing or GitHub Actions from remote source.

## 2026-11-07 — Runtime-authoritative quant advice and fail-closed gaming controller binding (SOURCE PARTIAL)

**Code-audited gaps:** Model Hub's automatic quantisation budget previously read `GpuDetected/VramTotalMib` even when Core had supplied a normalized `AcceleratorProfile` explicitly marking the GPU **ineligible** for local text inference. This could preselect a model as a full-GPU fit with no runnable backend. For UMA/integrated graphics, physical shared GPU aperture and system RAM were liable to be counted as independent memory. `VirtualPadActivationController.IdentityFor` reused `ListDevices()`, which caps UI enumeration at 16, to count same-VID/PID controllers before HidHide isolation. An identical controller beyond the first 16 could evade that count; a disconnected selected controller could be confused with a live duplicate. Start/stop exceptions also bypassed hot-plug unsubscription.

**Implemented directly on `main`:**
- `ModelHubQuantAdvice.BudgetFor` respects Core's normalized `AcceleratorEligible`, never inventing usable VRAM from a physically present but unsupported GPU. For normalized dedicated GPUs, caps measured free VRAM to that selected accelerator's advertised physical capacity. For `SharedMemoryPrimary`, sets the *separate* GPU budget to zero and uses only conservatively usable system RAM so it is not double-counted; quant selection is a **RAM-budget estimate**, not a promise of CPU-only execution. For eligible UMA model choices, clears false `CpuOnly` placement and adds explicit EN/IT caveat that launch and GPU speed need verification. When neither GPU nor RAM is measured, does not assert that no variant can fit. Manual model choice is never overwritten. Legacy Core without accelerator profile keeps the prior provisional GPU capacity path; no change to backend or auto-download.
- `VirtualPadActivationController` reconciles global WinRT hot-plug subscription in `finally` on explicit Start/Stop, including errors: retain it only when the virtual pad is genuinely running or waiting for manual re-arm. `WindowsRawGamepadSource.CountMatchingLiveIdentity` uses a one-off **complete** Windows raw controller inventory independent of the 16-entry *visual* list, and calls `PadBindingVerifier.CountMatchingRawControllers` to demand that the selected NonRoamableId is actually present and to reject duplicate VID/PID devices. Unknown, stale, or failed enumeration returns zero, so existing HID binding fails closed before hiding anything. Does not install/start ViGEmBus, HidHide or forwarding without explicit consent.
- Added deterministic tests in `ModelHubQuantAdviceTests` for ineligible GPU despite physical VRAM, UMA double-counting, unknown hardware and clamped measured free VRAM; `VirtualPadActivationTests` for stale selected controller and duplicate outside the visible first 16; source wiring in `ProfileSurfaceUiContractTests`.

**Remaining status: PARTIAL — no native tests run.** Must build/test on physical Windows: full solution + xUnit/FlaUI, physical NVIDIA eGPU with/without eligible CUDA backend, AMD/Intel UMA low-memory PC, GPU removal/replug, exact 8/16 GiB RAM headroom, model quant selection, after-load resident memory, latency and crash recovery; two identical gamepads, 17+ raw devices, unplug during setup, starting/stopping exceptions, HID/ViGEmBus consent and rollback, controller double-input prevention. Gaming FPS regression target under 2% remains **unmeasured**. Never claim exact inference quality/speed or install a driver automatically. No release/tag/actions/signing from cloud.

## 2026-11-07 — Standard/Portable/Android model discovery + large-gallery concurrency (SOURCE PARTIAL)

**Verified code issues:** Advanced Chat quick model comparison constructed TWO full WinUI ComboBoxes for every installed model, despite default Chat's already paged picker; 100–1000 installed GGUFs create unnecessary controls and obscure selections. Standard/Portable media gallery capped concurrent HTTP requests at four but still instantiated one Task per artifact using `Task.WhenAll(artifacts.Select(...))`, so thousands of items still allocated thousands of waiting tasks; overlapping Refresh calls could apply old results after a newer refresh. Generated-files directory enumeration also ran on WinUI thread. Android Chat's model menu had bounded 20 rows but did not offer file-size/speed sorting or quantization search.

**Implemented directly on `main` (SOURCE ONLY):**
- Reused `ChatModelPickerPolicy.Browse` for Advanced Chat **Quick Compare** with searchable 24-result pages, explicit selected A/B model-file identity, prompt/disjoint installed-file validation before primary confirmation, IT/EN text, 13-inch scrollable bounded UI, accessible automation IDs, and a speed label only when a valid local benchmark exists. Existing Arena Core execution/owner/Gaming gates, dual sequential inference and ordinary Chat model routing remain untouched. `CanCompare` rejects alias-based, missing or duplicate file identities.
- Added explicit catalogue ordering `ChatModelBrowseSort.Name/SmallestFileFirst/MeasuredSpeedFirst` in the default Chat browser and Advanced Compare. Unknown size or missing/invalid/NaN/Infinite benchmark goes last; local speed data is **historical observation**, not predicted latency, model quality or runtime hardware proof. Explicit manual selection remains authoritative.
- Android `MobileModelBrowsePolicy` brings the same deterministic 20-item bounded name/size/recorded-speed order and alias/filename/quantization search, preserves pinned manual filename when unfiltered, and never downloads/loads a model merely on browsing. Added pure Kotlin JUnit tests for 125 installed models, late selection, duplicates, unusual aliases, small-file and trustworthy measured-speed sorting.
- Media Gallery now loads artifact version metadata in groups of only 4 tasks, **not** creating a task per entire library; uses generation/CTS ownership to stop stale refreshes from overwriting the latest results, disposes the previous cancellation source only after its own task completes, and enumerates local generated files on a cancelable background thread to keep WinUI responsive. Existing filter/page/thumbnail limits remain.
- Updated `ChatModelPickerPolicyTests` and `ProfileSurfaceUiContractTests` for sort, compare selection, metadata batching, cancellation isolation and background scans. All test cases **authored, not executed**.

**Acceptance still BLOCKED-EXTERNAL:** .NET build + full and focused xUnit, exclusive FlaUI default model browser and advanced compare with 0/2/24/125/1000 installed models, keyboard/reader/13-inch + 4K high DPI; Android Gradle `testDebugUnitTest`, physical Compose search/sort on low-memory phones and pairing; gallery with 10k artifacts, cancellation/refresh race, corrupt individual metadata, slow/disconnected Core and UI-latency instrumentation; verify real model benchmark freshness on changed hardware, GPU/CPU launch and fallback, Gaming hard-off and FPS<2%. Avoid any new auto-load, background benchmark, unexpected network egress, release/tag/signature from remote.

**Next prioritized user-facing gaps:** measured hardware-fit labels and zero-terminal first-run model setup (no speculative recommendations); model load failure UX/retry and realistic tokens/s on 8–16 GiB PCs; image/video preview and recoverable generations; controller/TV Gaming journeys. Corporate remains isolated local document profile, not the center of standard AI/Gaming work.

## 2026-11-07 — Consumer Create / Gallery / Android jobs usability & resource safety (SOURCE PARTIAL)

**Verified root causes in current source:** `ChatMediaJobReconciler.Reconcile` treated the Core's legitimate `cancelling` state as an unknown terminal error; ongoing Chat media jobs persisted progress but did not show the engine's existing `EtaSeconds`. The standard `MediaGalleryView` eagerly rendered every generated thumbnail at source resolution and fetched Artifact Workspace version metadata sequentially. Android Create consumed chosen images with unbounded `readBytes()`, closed its draft dialog before Core admitted the job, showed stale/empty queues as if current, generated notification storms for historical completed jobs on first poll, and its jobs poller continued even after the tab was hidden.

**Changes committed to `main` (source only; standard MSI/Portable + Android):**
- Chat media recover/re-attach handles `queued/running/cancelling` as in-progress rather than a false failure. Shows stage, bounded 0–100% and **only** valid engine-provided ETA (1–86,400s) as an *approximate* label; clears ETA when step changes or settles. `ChatMessage.MediaEtaSeconds` is additive/backwards compatible; original image remains available after chained-video failure/cancel.
- Create Media Gallery searches/sorts all entries but instantiates just **24** WinUI image/video cards per page and decodes images to 320×220 thumbnail size rather than opening full 4K/8K source bitmaps; moving between pages and deletion clamp safely. Version metadata reads now have max four concurrent local HTTP requests, per-asset fallback and cancellation; do not conflate fewer UI cards with fewer already-indexed files.
- Android media editing only reads up to **8 MiB** from an arbitrary ContentProvider *before* base64 conversion. Failed/oversized/empty image selection is visible and clears the stale selected image. Jobs screen reflects Core pairing and stale-list errors rather than incorrectly declaring no jobs. Create dialog keeps prompt/image until server **accepts** the job, prevents duplicate submissions, and displays errors while preserving draft. Cancellation request errors are explicitly reported. Compose jobs polling stops on screen disposal (retained ViewModel must not poll during Gaming or when hidden).
- Android completion notifications now use a pure `JobCompletionNotificationPolicy`: initial successful sync establishes a baseline with no notifications for existing finished jobs; subsequent new completions notify once. No external push/FCM/cloud. Tests for first-sync/next-sync/duplicates are authored.

**Tests authored, NOT run:** expanded `ChatMediaJobReconcilerTests` for in-flight cancellation/ETA/legacy-JSON, `MediaGalleryBrowserTests` for 105 outputs, late page, filtered pagination and empty catalog; new Kotlin `JobCompletionNotificationPolicyTest.kt`; expanded `ProfileSurfaceUiContractTests` verifying gallery decode, concurrency, Android input bounds, submission and lifecycle. Source link/static inspection alone cannot prove decode memory, threading, Android Compose, reliability or FPS.

**Required native acceptance:** Windows .NET Contracts/Server/App build and full xUnit; focused `ChatMediaJobReconcilerTests|MediaGalleryBrowserTests|ProfileSurfaceUiContractTests`, exclusive FlaUI Create/Gallery on 13-inch and 4K/HDR high DPI, 100–10,000 media files, long video/partial completed image, connection drop, cancel while stopping, model unload/Gaming hard-off, measured cold-start, thumbnail heap and metadata-fetch latency. Android Gradle `testDebugUnitTest` (notification policy), emulator/real device Compose Create with 8 MiB boundary, zero-length/overlength provider, airplane-mode after submit and cancel, rotation, notification permission and blocked PC. Check 8/16 GiB RAM and FPS<2% while Gaming. No release/build/signing from remote connector. **Next priority:** native measured UI quality, generation preview persistence and server-side media preview/scale; controller/TV and model-fit are separate mandatory streams.

## 2026-11-07 — Consumer Chat history/Group Chat safety follow-on (SOURCE PARTIAL; native QA blocked)

**Scope:** standard MSI/Desktop, Portable, and shared model service used by the full PaRuX AI environment; no changes to Corporate restrictions or Gaming runtime ownership. Source-audited a high-risk prior condition: `HistoryStore.Open()` attempted destructive quarantine/recovery on *every* `SqliteException`, including temporary lock, full disk and permission/IO failures. Existing Group Chat also restored the default model after cancellation *before* obtaining the exclusive GPU lease, risking interference with another active model.

**Committed on main:**
- `HistoryBackupSafety.IsCorruptionErrorCode` only permits recovery on `SQLITE_CORRUPT` (11, including extended codes) or `SQLITE_NOTADB` (26), never on BUSY/LOCKED/IOERR/FULL/READONLY. `HistoryStore.OpenCore` disposes failed handles; recovery requires a real, verified nonempty snapshot **before** moving the database, moves DB/WAL/SHM to unique preserved paths, restores without overwrite, and best-effort rolls back moved files if copy fails. If no verified recovery exists, **do not** create a blank substitute or move original. Callers continue to handle/report failed opens.
- Daily-backup promotion uses `File.Replace` (same-volume atomic replacement with old-destination backup) instead of two sequential moves, so power loss cannot leave a missing canonical daily copy during replacement. Recovery candidate ranking prefers valid canonical `history-YYYY-MM-DD.db` over preserved copies of the same day, but can recover a `.previous-*.db` if canonical is missing after interruption. Pending/invalid names do not qualify.
- Group Chat validates max 12k user prompt and 12k explicitly pasted reference, rejects duplicate model file paths, passes only a 2k-char excerpt of each preceding model reply to the next model (original full response remains shown), and JSON-quotes untrusted pasted reference; user receives IT/EN duplicate/missing-input guidance without launching the GPU. Cancellation/failure before any model transition attempt **does not** restore default runtime, avoiding interference when still queued for an exclusive GPU lease.

**Tests authored:** `HistoryBackupSafetyTests` corruption primary/extended codes and non-corruption, interrupted canonical replacement and candidate rank; `GroupChatVerdictTests` max prompt, unique speaker files, previous-response bounds, untrusted context encoding; `ProfileSurfaceUiContractTests` source wiring. **Not run in this remote GitHub-only session.** Validate on native Windows: SQLite locked connection on concurrent process, full disk, corrupt file with/without backup, permissions, partial backup/atomic replace power interruption, Windows Store packaged filesystem rules, WAL safety, no silent data loss, Group Chat cancel before and after model load, 2–4 local models, single-GPU residency and Gaming hard-off. On mobile ensure Chat routing unaffected. GUI/disk/performance measurements still required; do not publish release or claim parity.

## 2026-11-07 — Standard / Portable / all-edition UX + Locally Uncensored 3.0.10 comparison (SOURCE PARTIAL)

**Direction:** PaRuX standard Desktop/MSI and Portable are the full, beginner-friendly local AI/creation/coding experience; Gaming/Console and Android are first-class editions with profile-specific controls. Corporate remains one strictly local, CPU-friendly general-purpose profile, **not** the definition of the whole product. Do not regress Gaming hard-off, permission ownership or default local-first model operation.

**Competitor verification (official GitHub, 2026-11-07):** Locally Uncensored v3.0.10 (2026-11-05) prioritizes conversation backup preservation; v3.0.9 exposes advances in multi-model group flow, small models on modest PCs, creation previews and training UI. Prior feature review: [`docs/COMPETITOR_PARITY_AUDIT_2026-11-06.md`](docs/COMPETITOR_PARITY_AUDIT_2026-11-06.md), now updated with this follow-up. Other vendors and parity remain an **evidence-driven backlog**; source presence is not measured parity.

**Android standard follow-on:** `ChatScreen.ModelSelectorRow` now searches installed local model filename/alias and bounds Compose menu rows to 20, keeping an explicitly pinned current manual model visible even if outside the initial results; displays honest truncation/refine-search notice rather than allocating hundreds of menu rows. Native Android UI/Gradle and accessibility remain **unverified**.

**Code on `main` in this batch (NOT Windows-verified):**
- **Standard/Portable Chat model UX:** `ChatModelPickerPolicy` bounds the quick flyout to twelve installed models while keeping an explicitly pinned manual choice visible; a separate search/paginated 24-model browser filters alias, filename and quantization, preserves existing authoritative Core/ComboBox selection, does not load/download a model on browse, and blocks confirmation until an actual row is chosen. IT/EN, native UI automation names and an honest missing-pin indicator.
- **History integrity (standard + Portable):** `HistoryBackupSafety` verifies SQLite daily backup quick-check + nonempty conversation table before recovery, ignores corrupt/empty archives, never overwrites a validated backup and archives any invalid previous daily destination before promoting a checked staged copy. `HistoryStore` skips empty startup snapshots and attempts the first nonempty daily snapshot after a durable Save; the live database is only quarantined on an actual SQLite open failure, not simply because it is empty. No automatic restoring of intentionally deleted chats. This **hardens** pre-existing backups; it does not assert PaRuX had the exact competitor bug or verify every JSON message.
- **Standard Group Chat:** existing sequential 2–4 local-model panel accepts an owner-entered optional shared reference (max 12k chars) used by all speakers and verdict, labeled untrusted data. ## 2026-11-07 — Corporate >1,000 sources + fast folder management (SOURCE PARTIAL; QA BLOCKED-EXTERNAL)

**Priority and problem verified in current code:** legacy `KnowledgeIndex.AppendSourceRestriction` refused >900 source IDs, so large all-enabled Corporate registries could yield empty/no answers; `KnowledgeJobService.HasData` issued two SQLite COUNT queries per registered folder, and `ScopedSourceIds` used a quadratic list membership check. `DocumentSearchView` previously rendered every folder row and eagerly requested every status. Frequent first invoice matches could also crowd out rare second-invoice evidence from a small top-k comparison.

**Committed on `main` (source only):** bound one serialized JSON source-ID array to SQLite `json_each` inside the FTS/scan/semantic SQL source restriction, before `LIMIT`. This preserves source isolation and supports special characters, with a native JSON1 capability gate. A new single-query `KnowledgeIndex.HasAnyIndexedChunks` replaces per-folder Stats probes; `ScopedSourceIds` uses HashSet membership. `SearchExact` reserves up to one genuinely present passage per distinct requested code when the number of codes fits top-k, then fills remaining slots by ranking; missing identifiers still cause grounded abstention in Document Chat. **This is not ANN:** semantic vector scan remains O(N), no performance numbers measured.

**UI/UX:** `DocumentSearchView` reuses the shared `DocumentSourcePicker` to render 40 management rows per page with IT/EN name/path filter and 280ms debounce, visible-only source status fetches, active-only polling, hidden-page cancellation, disabled-for-Chat label and no change to registered source settings. Chat's picker still excludes disabled folders. Android observes Core pairing live and shows a disconnected banner; short text drafts use `rememberSaveable` across Activity recreation without persisting 8 MiB image base64 in savedInstanceState. Standard Windows/Portable share the same views; Console/TV and native focus **not verified**.

**Tests authored, NOT RUN:** `KnowledgeIndexTests` native JSON1 gate, 1,251-source explicit allowlist (quoted, Unicode, backslash IDs), negative scope, single-query readiness, frequent/rare multi-invoice exact top-3; `DocumentSourcePickerTests` management visibility vs Chat exclusion; 43-source pagination/filter FlaUI scenario; `ProfileSurfaceUiContractTests` for search, disabled folders and Android pairing/draft. The Corporate QA protocol now specifies benchmark and high-DPI/keyboard/Android rotation acceptance.

**Mandatory next gates:** native Windows Contracts/Server/App build + focused/full xUnit (`KnowledgeIndexTests|DocumentSourcePickerTests|KnowledgeLksTests|ProfileSurfaceUiContractTests`), exclusive FlaUI `DocumentSearchUiTests`, Android Gradle/device/UI/rotation; test 1k–10k registered folders, SQLite query latency and allocations, original-source removal and no leakage, 30k-page/10k-file 8/16 GiB offline workloads, cross-domain citation and missing-evidence scoring, UI focus/dynamic filters, 13-inch high DPI, TV/controller and Gaming hard-off. Native FTS5/JSON1 and memory measurements remain unverified from GitHub cloud. No release, artifact/signature, tag or Actions. **Next highest impact P1:** ANN/vector partitioning, OCR/table/figure extraction evidence and source-version authority, not speculative perfect-answer claims.

## 2026-11-06 — Corporate technical document assistant / 30,000-page PDF scale (P0; SOURCE PARTIAL / WINDOWS UNVERIFIED)

**User objective:** Corporate Lite CPU-friendly, strictly local technical assistant over thousands of documents, including single 30,000-page PDFs, with reliable answers grounded in explicit source/version/page/section, understandable import/progress/coverage and fast exact DID/DTC/engineering-parameter retrieval. Never promise zero hallucinations; enforce abstention on unsupported claims and measure error/recall.

**Source audit (code > tests > runtime):** Original `KnowledgeIndex` had per-file extraction into a `List<DocumentSegment>`, Eco 256 MiB file and 5M-character text caps; thus big manuals were truncated or skipped. Source discovery is already recursive, protected, incremental, and position-bearing, but `SearchSemantic` scans all stored vectors (O(N)) and source restrictions were applied after FTS LIMIT, losing selected-folder hits at large scale. Legacy RAG reindex also started a second full extractor alongside Knowledge Index. PDF tables, drawings, OCR accuracy, full per-claim verification and giant-document diff remain specialized risks.

**P0 source implemented this tranche:**
- `KnowledgeIndex.LargePdf.cs` adds out-of-core PDF text-layer parsing and opt-in Windows OCR for blank pages in 16-page batches (not the old 60-page OCR cap), SHA-bound persisted SQLite page/ordinal checkpoints, resume after shutdown/cancellation, bounded page buffers, exact page offsets and optional batched embeddings. A page >250k extracted characters fails visibly instead of truncating. 2 GiB hard file cap remains; no claim that >2 GiB files fit.
- `kn_pdf_progress` + `kn_pdf_staging` hold nonsearchable provisional text, while the old searchable index survives until a single final SQLite transaction publishes the entire new document + FTS/trigram; changed SHA or OCR mode clears stale checkpoints, missing OCR content is counted as unindexed, and source removal/entire-library wipe clears staged sensitive text securely. Earlier partial PDF indexes migrate to the paged path on next scan. Retiring an old giant FTS index uses 256-row slices instead of accumulating millions of rows.
- Knowledge API now reports true `CurrentPage`/`TotalPages` and the existing Settings UI shows determinate PDF progress and current document; no false percentage for folder enumeration. Corporate's old `/api/v1/rag/sources/{id}/reindex` now schedules only the authoritative Knowledge Index instead of TWO competing extractors; desktop owner gate, honest `backgroundIndexing` response and UI status. Standard edition keeps existing legacy behavior.
- FTS5 exact/lexical ranking, scan fallback and semantic retrieval now restrict selected source IDs inside parameterized SQLite queries before LIMIT/embedding scan, not after. Semantic scoring uses byte spans without allocating a second float[] per chunk, but still O(N) for full semantic search; ANN/vector partitioning is **P1 MISSING**.
- `DocumentChatPlanner` refuses metadata-only/filename-only pseudo-evidence, treats retrieved text as untrusted data not assistant instructions, requires source citations per claim, precise technical identifiers, units, revision/vehicle/ECU conditions, conflict warnings, and abstention for absent values. It cannot guarantee all generated claims are correct without independent evaluation. `/diff` refuses giant whole-file comparisons beyond 12,000 chunks rather than silently truncating or exhausting memory; streaming section-aware diff is **P1 MISSING**.

**Authored tests not executed:** `KnowledgeLargePdfTests` (48-page last-page citation after Eco caps, page checkpoint progress, cancellation/resume, atomic old/new visibility, stage secure deletion, OCR opt-in/reconfiguration); `KnowledgeIndexTests` selected-folder topK exact/lexical regression against 80 out-of-scope matches; `KnowledgeLksTests` technical pseudo-evidence refusal and p.27568 citation. No 30k-page or 10k-file runtime benchmark performed; tests and source code are NOT Windows-verified.

**Acceptance gates (P0 BLOCKED-EXTERNAL until Windows):** build Contracts/Server/App; run focused/full xUnit and exclusive FlaUI; load a genuine 30,000-page text-layer manual (and a scanned/mixed-language sample), verify late-page DID and page citation, zero unintended omissions, 2 GiB cap messaging, crash/restart at page 16/16000/29984, folder removal/secure wipe, OCR enabled/disabled and stale state migration; test 5k–50k mixed technical files in resource-constrained Corporate 8/16 GB, publish actual median/p95 exact/semantic latencies, documents/s, peak managed RAM, CPU %, SQLite index size, disk-full handling, pause/stop responsiveness, background indexing and no-network egress. Proposed targets for measurement (not claims): exact warm FTS query p95 <=2 s on 10k files; no OOM at 30k pages; no incorrect/no-source technical answers in the negative benchmark. Test citations against conflicting document revisions; do not emit definitive parameters when year/ECU applicability is missing.

**Remaining priority gaps:** P0 security correctness QA, progressive indexing under disk pressure, OCR quality and per-page failure evidence, user-friendly add-folder + searchable readiness; P1 persistent ANN/partitioned vector search for >100k chunks and CPU-limited PCs, per-claim citation cross-check, document variant/version authority, PDF table/diagram OCR, page/section-scoped streaming comparison and document-level summaries, adaptive indexing scheduler. Release only from authorized Windows PC after hardware and signed artifact tests.

### 2026-11-06 — Training parity tranche 3: KTO binary feedback, completion-only SFT, API admission (PARTIAL — CODE COMMITTED / WINDOWS UNVERIFIED)

**Implemented on main, source only:** KTO is the eighth accelerated training method, using the already pinned TRL 0.24 `KTOTrainer/KTOConfig` with genuine JSONL `{prompt:string, completion:string, label:boolean}`; no fake generated negatives or free-form reward-code execution. The existing bounded dataset inspector counts positive/negative records and the preflight shows class balance, blocks one-sided feedback and fewer than 4 examples, warns for severe imbalance. GPU job admission streams the *entire* KTO JSONL (including after the 2,000-row preview), rejects malformed or nonboolean labels, and checks KTO's minimum **physical micro-batch 4**. This is not interchangeable with gradient accumulation: the runner uses 4 physical x 4 accumulation = effective batch 16, per TRL 0.24 guidance; training selects a cautious **1e-6** starting LR at beta 0.1 and bounded reweighting of the minority feedback class. Hardware fit changing before execution produces a durable failed job, not an orphaned queue. The `TrainingBatchShapePolicy` is now shared by preview/v1/v2 admission and runner, so a context-length/VRAM combination that cannot fit four physical KTO examples is blocked before queueing. KTO remains hidden in Quick and unsupported on CPU/Corporate, but available in Advanced + CLI + runtime catalog. Added source-contract and pure xUnit coverage; **not executed**.

**Model quality fix:** conversational SFT now converts each genuine assistant response into a TRL conversational `prompt`/`completion` training row, preserving all earlier turns and explicitly setting `completion_only_loss=True`; prior `text` training accidentally computed loss on user/system instruction tokens. Continued pretraining retains `{text}` and whole-sequence loss. Inspected chat data must include a nonempty assistant response after a real user turn. This uses prompt/completion masking rather than assuming every tokenizer implements generation-mask chat tags; actual token masks, packed sequences and adapter quality still need native tests.

**API/UX:** `/api/v2/training/jobs` now calls the same memory/shape planner as v1/UI before running and blocks configurations that cannot fit, including 16-bit LoRA; it still runs the full TrainingLabService validation and backend status gate. Advanced UI explains KTO JSONL classes, 4x4 batch and LR, renders observed class balance, CLI documents the new method, and adapter lineage uses truthful KTO labeling.

**Required Windows/physical acceptance:** compile Contracts/Intelligence/Server/App/CLI; focused/full xUnit and exclusive FlaUI; real managed Unsloth KTO run on the RTX 5080 with balanced and skewed binary feedback, checkpoint/resume, cancellation and Gaming hard-off; confirm actual 4x4 batch, reward metrics, minority-class weighting and no implicit model duplicate/VRAM overflow. Verify SFT completion_mask labels are -100 over all system/user tokens for several chat-template model families, including packed multi-turn training and one- and multi-turn sample equivalence; compare held-out eval vs old all-token runner. Exercise v2 API direct 400/409 returns and Corporate denial. No packages upgraded or releases/tags created. Do **not** claim throughput or quality parity/superiority to Unsloth without controlled same-GPU benchmarks.

### 2026-11-06 — Real profile gaps and Corporate CPU readiness repaired (SOURCE IMPLEMENTED; WINDOWS UNVERIFIED)

Continued directly on `main` with no branches, release, GitHub Actions or external runtime installers. This tranche repairs behavior that was not fixed by hiding top-level navigation:

**Setup-sensitive UI:** `SettingsSectionVisibilityPolicy` filters in-category cards, not merely menu categories. Corporate excludes skill authoring, the legacy floating bubble, extra model/remote settings and media tools; Gaming-only excludes Chat history import, game-to-Chat guide, AI asset generation and Return-to-AI controls when the AI runtime was never started. A stale saved Gaming-only setup from a shared data folder cannot hide Corporate first-run Chat controls; no data preference is overwritten.

**Actual Corporate CPU runtime (critical):** `RuntimeManagerService.GetStatusAsync` now publishes the bundled `text-cpu` backend in Corporate without probing GPU VRAM or listing incompatible CUDA/Vulkan/ComfyUI backends. `ComponentInventoryService.DiscoverAsync` returns one read-only `llama-server-cpu` component with no install/skip actions; `RuntimeManagerService.GetDiagnosticsAsync` reports only CPU/model issues instead of false CUDA/media/uncensored warnings. ### 2026-11-02 continuation XXI — race-free game download worker admission

**Public main: SOURCE IMPLEMENTED / TESTS AUTHORED NOT RUN.** The GameDownloadManager scheduler previously deferred reading `next.Run.Token` into a queued Task.Run lambda. A fast Pause/Cancel could cancel and dispose the mutable CTS before the lambda executed, producing an unobserved exception and a permanently stale running entry. The token is now captured before scheduling, and the worker checks Gaming/cancellation before any transfer preparation. Enqueue and Gaming-paused resume recheck hard-off under the queue lock (including explicit caller cancellation) to avoid accepting queued jobs across a concurrent Gaming entry. Transfer also checks hard-off before touching partial download files or issuing a network request.

- Three synthetic xUnit tests authored: deterministic Gaming admission race between checks, cancellation of a resume request, rapid job cancellation. **Tests, .NET build, Windows network and hardware NOT RUN.** The rapid cancellation test is a regression guard, not a deterministic proof of every scheduler interleaving.
- Windows gate: `dotnet test tests/PaRuX.Server.Tests/PaRuX.Server.Tests.csproj --filter "FullyQualifiedName~GameDownloadManagerTests"`; repeat Gaming entry during active streaming and queued worker scheduling; inspect CPU/disk/network after hard-off and resume only when AI-ready. No new pollers, downloads, installer execution, drivers or dependencies.

### 2026-11-02 continuation XVIII — bounded interrupted-package recovery

**Public main: SOURCE IMPLEMENTED / TESTS AUTHORED NOT RUN.** After an interrupted package-staging copy, exact `.parux-game-<32 lowercase hex>.partial` files may reserve the staging quota indefinitely. The next explicit Stage action now recovers only exact-named, unlinked partials older than 24 hours while holding the existing cross-process lease. ### 2026-11-02 continuation XVII — immutable cancellation token for instant-exit TV launch

**Public main: SOURCE IMPLEMENTED / NOT RUN.** The direct-game launcher now captures a `CancellationToken` before scheduling the exit observer, and passes the captured token to optional selected-TV placement. An instantly exiting .exe can complete the observer and dispose its `CancellationTokenSource` before the placement call is evaluated; the old `stop.Token` property access could throw `ObjectDisposedException` and cause a misleading "game started but automatic return unavailable" response. The immutable captured token remains valid after source disposal; the placement service still fails closed for an exited process. No additional polling, driver or display mutation.

- Validate on Windows with direct games and a synthetic executable that exits immediately, with selected TV and without; verify launch-result messages and manual Return. **No compilation, xUnit, FlaUI or hardware execution in this session.**

### 2026-11-02 continuation XV — fail-closed owned game child guard on verified process exit

**Public main: SOURCE IMPLEMENTED / TESTS AUTHORED NOT RUN.** When an owned direct-executable or emulator process exits after the existing 5-second bootstrap grace, the automatic AI/TV Home return now performs **one** Windows Toolhelp process snapshot. A still-running **direct child**, created during that exact verified process's lifetime, vetoes automatic resource recovery: Gaming stays active until explicit user Return. Unavailable snapshots, unreadable potential children, process identity uncertainty or cancellation also fail closed. There is no process kill, adoption, background poller, driver, elevation, or Steam/Epic/GOG auto-return.

- Five pure xUnit tests cover a live direct child, unrelated processes/PID reuse, incomplete/unreadable snapshots, no children, and irrelevant unreadable processes. The monitor now carries verified OS start time rather than re-reading it from an exited process. No Windows SDK, compiler, .NET tests, emulator, controller or game has been run.
- **Bounded limitation:** Toolhelp snapshots observe currently live *direct* children only, not a detached grandchild whose intermediate parent already exited. For Steam/Epic/GOG handoffs and unverifiable process trees, explicit Return remains required. This is a conservative risk reduction, not a claim of complete process-tree ownership.
- Windows checks: `dotnet test tests/PaRuX.Server.Tests/PaRuX.Server.Tests.csproj --filter "FullyQualifiedName~OwnedGameChildExitGuardTests|FullyQualifiedName~GamingTransitionConcurrencyTests"`; build Server and WinUI; test a normal direct executable, a 6+ second bootstrapper leaving a live child, denied child access, PID reuse, and canceled/manual Return. ### 2026-11-02 continuation XII — typed emulator game-file preflight and backward compatibility

**Public `main`: SOURCE IMPLEMENTED / TESTS AUTHORED NOT RUN.** New emulator imports persist a typed, optional `GameFilePath` alongside the executable and command line in the existing version-1 manual library. The selected path is validated as a regular local file of a format supported by that emulator during import and rechecked before launching the process, including immediately after the AI-to-Gaming resource transition. A moved, deleted, linked or unsupported game file fails closed; on failure after entering Gaming, the existing no-process rollback restores state. No game file is downloaded, copied or parsed.

### 2026-11-01 continuation VI — selected-TV routing for verified local game windows

**Status: SOURCE IMPLEMENTED / NOT RUN on public `main`.** No Windows/.NET SDK is available to this execution environment. - **Core service:** Added `OwnedGameWindowPlacementService`, a one-shot (about 5 seconds maximum) Win32 top-level window observation. The only allowed PID is that of a directly launched, previously verified manual game/emulator process (same process handle and image identity). The service refuses ambiguous multiple windows, child/owned/tool/invisible/minimized/maximized/frameless windows, missing or oversized windows and unsupported exclusive fullscreen. It does not inspect, move or terminate arbitrary windows or child processes.
- **TV routing:** The authenticated `POST /api/v1/gaming/library/{id}/launch` accepts an optional `displayId` query from the existing PaRuX desktop client. Server validates that the Windows display is still connected **before** entering Gaming and rechecks topology/owned-process/window identity during routing. The target is a user-specified Windows `DISPLAYn`, never a remote-provided HWND or executable. The native `SetWindowPos` call requests only the position of one verified compatible window using `SWP_NOACTIVATE|SWP_NOZORDER|SWP_NOOWNERZORDER|SWP_NOSIZE|SWP_ASYNCWINDOWPOS`; post-move window bounds and process PID are read again before reporting success, without hanging Core on a non-responsive GUI thread. It never changes primary-monitor choice, desktop topology, DPI, resolution, refresh, HDR, VRR or display duplication. Its position calculation supports negative virtual-desktop coordinates.
- **UI and prefs:** Added `AppSettings.RouteVerifiedWindowedGamesToTv` (default true; effective only when an explicit TV output is selected), independently switchable off in Settings > Gaming & AI Sharing. The preference remains available whether the launch begins in Desktop or Console. Both Desktop and Console local-game launches pass the display hint only when this preference and the destination are enabled; manual applications and Steam/Epic/GOG launch handoffs do not. The prelaunch confirmation discloses this behavior, and the launch result reports `placed`, `already-on-tv`, `unsupported-window`, `not-confirmed`, or `display-unavailable` rather than claiming every fullscreen game works.
- **Focused tests authored, NOT RUN:** `OwnedGameWindowPlacementPolicyTests` uses synthetic top-level window candidates (PID/visibility/owner/caption/ambiguity), geometry with non-primary/negative-position monitors, oversized-window rejection, and backward-compatible JSON launch receipts. `ManualGameLaunchSecurityTests` checks that display hints do not bypass the local desktop-owner authorization boundary.
- **Hardware gate:** Build Contracts/Core/WinUI App on Windows; run `dotnet test tests/PaRuX.Server.Tests/PaRuX.Server.Tests.csproj --filter "FullyQualifiedName~OwnedGameWindowPlacementPolicyTests|FullyQualifiedName~ManualGameLaunchTests|FullyQualifiedName~GamingDisplayTopologyTests"`; run configured UI FlaUI Gaming settings and console tests. Verify TV/PC dual screen and mixed DPI with a windowed direct game, Eden/Cemu/PCSX2 windowed and fullscreen, DRM-store handoff (must NOT move unowned windows), abort/replug TV, concurrent game launch, user Return to AI and game exit. Check that no frame-rate/VRAM regressions or focus theft occur.

The service re-checks cancellation after the quantizer returns and immediately before publishing the output. A job-scoped GamingHardOffWatch also reacts to the Gaming marker if activation occurs outside the standard orchestrator path; no idle or permanent poller is started.
